    <tr><td> Halo/subhalo_ids
    </td><td> -
    </td><td> The <a href="https://www.tng-project.org/data/docs/faq/#gen3">Subhalo ID</a> in the original TNG300-1 subhalo catalog.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Halo/M_star_in_2xR_star
    </td><td> -
    </td><td> Stellar mass within twice the stellar half mass radius.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Halo/X_halo
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Projected x-coordinate of the halo. Note: do not use for cross-matching with X-ray events (use RA, DEC, LOS for cross-matching on the sky).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Halo/Y_halo
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Projected y-coordinate of the halo. Note: do not use for cross-matching with X-ray events (use RA, DEC, LOS for cross-matching on the sky).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Halo/Z_halo
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Projected z-coordinate of the halo. Note: do not use for cross-matching with X-ray events (use RA, DEC, LOS for cross-matching on the sky).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Halo/flag_mass
    </td><td> -
    </td><td> This flag is <code>False</code> if SUBFIND did not measure a mass for this halo.
    </td></tr>

  </table>
</div>

<div style="text-align:center;">
  <table class="table table-bordered table-condensed eoTable">
    <tr>
    <th style='width:20%'> Dataset Name </th>
    <th style='width:12%'> Units </th>
    <th> Description </th>
    </tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/Subhalo_ID
    </td><td> -
    </td><td> A unique index for this subhalo.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/RA
    </td><td> deg
    </td><td> Right Ascension (J2000).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/DEC
    </td><td> deg
    </td><td> Declination (J2000).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/Parent_ID
    </td><td> -
    </td><td> The unique ID of the parent halo (the <code>Halo_ID</code> field above).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/M_star_in_2xR_star
    </td><td> $10^{10} \rm{M}_\odot$
    </td><td> Stellar mass within twice the stellar half mas radius.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/Cen_M_star_in_2xR_star
    </td><td> $10^{10} \rm{M}_\odot$
    </td><td> Stellar mass of the corresponding central halo, within twice the stellar half mas radius.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/subhalo_sfr
    </td><td> $\rm{M}_\odot / \rm{yr}$
    </td><td> Star formation rate.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/stellar_metallicitiy
    </td><td> -
    </td><td> Stellar metallicity.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/subhalo_radius
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Comoving radius (stellar, or total, half mass radius?).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/subhalo_radius_deg
    </td><td> deg
    </td><td> Angular size corresponding to <code>subhalo_radius</code>.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/D_c
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Comoving distance to the subhalo.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/redshift
    </td><td> -
    </td><td> Redshift of the subhalo within the lightcone.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/M500c
    </td><td> $10^{10} \rm{M}_\odot$
    </td><td> Parent halo mass (see definitions above).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/M200m
    </td><td> $10^{10} \rm{M}_\odot$
    </td><td> Parent halo mass (see definitions above).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/M200c
    </td><td> $10^{10} \rm{M}_\odot$
    </td><td> Parent halo mass (see definitions above).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/R200c
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Comoving radius of the parent halo (see definitions above).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/R200m
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Comoving radius of the parent halo (see definitions above).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/R500c
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Comoving radius of the parent halo (see definitions above).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/R200c_deg
    </td><td> deg
    </td><td> Angular size corresponding to R200c.
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/X_halo
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Projected x-coordinate of the subhalo. Note: do not use for cross-matching with X-ray events (use RA, DEC, LOS for cross-matching on the sky).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/Y_halo
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Projected y-coordinate of the subhalo. Note: do not use for cross-matching with X-ray events (use RA, DEC, LOS for cross-matching on the sky).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/Z_halo
    </td><td> ckpc
    </td><td> Projected z-coordinate of the subhalo. Note: do not use for cross-matching with X-ray events (use RA, DEC, LOS for cross-matching on the sky).
    </td></tr>

    <tr><td> Subhalo/flag_mass
    </td><td> -
    </td><td> This flag is <code>False</code> if SUBFIND did not measure a mass for the (parent) subhalo.
    </td></tr>

  </table>
</div>
